# IMPERIA COLD WAR — France 1970 bilateral diplomacy.
COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_pompidou_europe = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_send_pompidou_europe = yes }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_pompidou_europe
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 180 }
		GER = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_pompidou_europe = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_pompidou_europe = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		GER = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		57 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		GER = {
		42 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_pompidou_europe = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0004 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_pompidou_europe_france_weekly_share
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france
		FRA = {
			57 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_recipient_heading
	GER = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_recipient = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0004 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_recipient_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe_quote_weekly_recipient multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_pompidou_europe_recipient_weekly_share
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER
		GER = {
			42 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_pompidou_europe = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_pompidou_europe = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_pompidou_europe western_conservatism_are_in_power = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_pompidou_europe } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_pompidou_europe } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_pompidou_europe = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_send_atlantic_liberal = yes }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_atlantic_liberal
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 180 }
		USA = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_atlantic_liberal = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_liberal
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_atlantic_liberal set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_liberal
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		USA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		USA = {
		811 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_france_research_2 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		USA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_recipient_research_3 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.25 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_atlantic_liberal = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0005 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.003 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_atlantic_liberal_france_weekly_share
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_atlantic_liberal_france
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_liberal_france_research_2 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_atlantic_liberal = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_liberal } }

	}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_atlantic_security = {

	}
}

	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_atlantic_security neutrality_neutral_conservatism_are_in_power = yes NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_atlantic_security } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_party_atlantic_security } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_party_atlantic_security } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_atlantic_security } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_atlantic_security = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_independent_communism = {

		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_independent_communism value = 1 days = 180 }

	}
}

	}

	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0004 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_us_science_france_weekly_share
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_france
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_france_research_1 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_recipient_heading
	USA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_recipient = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0004 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_recipient_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_quote_weekly_recipient multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0008 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_us_science_recipient_weekly_share
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_USA
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science_recipient_research_1 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_us_science = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_us_science FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_us_science } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_us_science
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_us_science value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_us_science clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_civil_science set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_us_science value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_us_science = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_us_science  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_us_science } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_civil_science } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_civil_science } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_us_science } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_us_science = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_algeria_energy = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_send_algeria_energy = yes }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_mediterranean_energy value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_algeria_energy
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_algeria_energy value = 1 days = 180 }
		ALG = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_algeria_energy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_algeria_energy = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_algeria_energy
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_algeria_energy set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_mediterranean_energy clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_mediterranean_energy clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_algeria_energy clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_algeria_energy
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_algeria_energy
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		ALG = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.001 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_senegal_boards
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_senegal_boards value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_senegal_boards clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_finance_0 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_senegal_boards value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_senegal_boards = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_senegal_boards  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_senegal_boards } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_african_finance_0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_finance_0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_senegal_boards } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_senegal_boards = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_cameroon_guarantee = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_send_fp_cameroon_guarantee = yes }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_finance_1 value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_fp_cameroon_guarantee
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_cameroon_guarantee value = 1 days = 180 }
		CAM = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_accept_fp_cameroon_guarantee = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_fp_cameroon_guarantee = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_cameroon_guarantee
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_cameroon_guarantee set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_african_finance_1 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_finance_1 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_cameroon_guarantee clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_cameroon_guarantee
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_cameroon_guarantee
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		CAM = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		56 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		CAM = {
		321 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_france_research_3 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		CAM = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_recipient_research_3 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_fp_cameroon_guarantee = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00001 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0025 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_cameroon_guarantee_france_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_france_0 = { value = CAM.gdp_total multiply = 0.002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_france_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_france_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_cameroon_guarantee_france_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_france
		FRA = {
			56 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_france_research_3 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_recipient_heading
	CAM = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_recipient = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_recipient_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_weekly_recipient multiply = 1000 } }

		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_cameroon_guarantee_recipient_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_recipient_0 = { value = CAM.gdp_total multiply = 0.002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_recipient_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_quote_transfer_recipient_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_cameroon_guarantee_recipient_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_CAM
		CAM = {
			321 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_cameroon_guarantee_recipient_research_3 category = CAT_electronics bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_niger_field_services = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_field_services FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_field_services } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_field_services
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_field_services value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_field_services clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_security_3 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_field_services value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_niger_field_services = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_niger_field_services  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_field_services } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_african_security_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_security_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_field_services } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_niger_field_services = yes
	}
}

		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_security_3 value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_fp_niger_rural
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_rural value = 1 days = 180 }
		NGR = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_rural value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_rural
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_rural COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_fp_niger_rural = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_niger_rural
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_niger_rural set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_african_security_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_african_security_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_rural clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_rural }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_niger_rural
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_niger_rural
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		NGR = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		59 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		NGR = {
		331 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 2
			add_building_construction = {
				type = agriculture_district
				level = 2
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		331 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_niger_rural_france_research_3 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		NGR = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_niger_rural_recipient_research_4 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }

		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_fp_egypt_aviation = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_egypt_aviation
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_egypt_aviation set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_0 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_0 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_egypt_aviation clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_egypt_aviation
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.004 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		EGY = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0035 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		60 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		EGY = {
		216 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = air_base
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation_france_research_3 category = CAT_aircraft bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		EGY = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation_recipient_research_3 category = CAT_aircraft bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
		add_doctrine_cost_reduction = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation_recipient_doctrine_4 category = air_doctrine cost_reduction = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation_quote_transfer_recipient_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation_quote_transfer_recipient_0 multiply = 1000 } }

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_egypt_aviation = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_egypt_aviation value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_0 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_egypt_aviation value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_egypt_aviation = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_egypt_aviation  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_egypt_aviation } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_0 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_egypt_aviation } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_egypt_aviation = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_saudi_naval = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_naval FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_naval } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_naval
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_naval value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_naval clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_2 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_naval value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_saudi_naval = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_saudi_naval  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_saudi_naval } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_2 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_2 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_saudi_naval } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_saudi_naval = yes
	}
}

		limit = {
			has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery
			FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_accept_fp_lebanon_recovery = yes }
		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_lebanon_recovery
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_lebanon_recovery set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_3 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_recovery clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_recovery
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		LEB = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		63 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		LEB = {
		202 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		202 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_france_research_3 category = CAT_infrastructure bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		LEB = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_recipient_research_4 category = CAT_infrastructure bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_fp_lebanon_recovery = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00001 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0045 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_lebanon_recovery_france_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_france_0 = { value = FRA.gdp_total multiply = 0.00025 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_france_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_france_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_lebanon_recovery_france_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_france
		FRA = {
			63 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_france_research_3 category = CAT_infrastructure bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_recipient_heading
	LEB = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_recipient = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_recipient_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_weekly_recipient multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.002 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_lebanon_recovery_recipient_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_recipient_0 = { value = FRA.gdp_total multiply = 0.00025 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_recipient_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_quote_transfer_recipient_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_lebanon_recovery_recipient_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_LEB
		LEB = {
			202 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		LEB = {
			202 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery_recipient_research_4 category = CAT_infrastructure bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_lebanon_recovery = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_recovery value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_3 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_recovery value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_lebanon_recovery = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_lebanon_recovery  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_lebanon_recovery } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_arab_contracts_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_arab_contracts_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_lebanon_recovery } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_lebanon_recovery = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_india_aviation = {
	if = {
		limit = { COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_can_send_fp_india_aviation = yes }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_india_aviation value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_0 value = 1 days = 90 }
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_ever_sent_fp_india_aviation
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_india_aviation value = 1 days = 180 }
		RAJ = { set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_india_aviation value = 1 days = 90 } }
	}
}

		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_vietnam_engineering set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_global_partnerships_1 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_1 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_vietnam_engineering clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_vietnam_engineering
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.004 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		VIE = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes

		58 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1

				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		VIE = {
		522 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = industrial_complex
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes

				type = infrastructure
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {

	}
		VIE = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_recipient_research_4 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
		FRA = { COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = yes }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_preview_fp_vietnam_engineering = {
	custom_effect_tooltip = TT_IF_THEY_ACCEPT
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_france_accept_heading
	FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_france = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.00001 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_france_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_france multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.004 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_vietnam_engineering_france_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_france_0 = { value = FRA.gdp_total multiply = 0.0002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_france_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_france_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_vietnam_engineering_france_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_france
		FRA = {
			58 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = offices
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_france_research_3 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
	custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_recipient_heading
	VIE = {
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_recipient = { value = gdp_total multiply = 0.0002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_recipient_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_weekly_recipient multiply = 1000 } }
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_vietnam_engineering_recipient_weekly_share
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_recipient_0 = { value = FRA.gdp_total multiply = 0.0002 divide = 52 } }
		set_temp_variable = { COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_recipient_0_millions = { value = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_quote_transfer_recipient_0 multiply = 1000 } }
		custom_effect_tooltip = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_vietnam_engineering_recipient_weekly_transfer_0
		add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering_VIE
		VIE = {
			522 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		VIE = {

				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1

					type = infrastructure
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes

	}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_vietnam_engineering = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_vietnam_engineering value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_1 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_vietnam_engineering value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_vietnam_engineering = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_vietnam_engineering  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_vietnam_engineering } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_global_partnerships_1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_1 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_vietnam_engineering } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_vietnam_engineering = yes
	}
}

		}
		set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_mauritius_marine
		FRA = { set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_fp_mauritius_marine set_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_global_partnerships_2 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_2 clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_mauritius_marine clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_mauritius_marine }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_mauritius_marine
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_mauritius_marine
		FRA = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.004 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		MRT = {
		set_temp_variable = { treasury_change = { value = gdp_total multiply = -0.0015 } }
		modify_treasury_effect = yes
	}
		FRA = {
		64 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		MRT = {
		270 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = dockyard
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
		270 = {
			add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
			add_building_construction = {
				type = offices
				level = 1
				instant_build = yes
			}
		}
	}
		FRA = {
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_mauritius_marine_france_research_3 category = CAT_naval bonus = 0.5 uses = 1 }
	}
		MRT = {

	}

					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		DJI = {
			230 = {
				add_extra_state_shared_building_slots = 1
				add_building_construction = {
					type = industrial_complex
					level = 1
					instant_build = yes
				}
			}
		}
		add_tech_bonus = { name = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_djibouti_civil_recipient_research_4 category = CAT_industry bonus = 0.5 uses = 2 }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_decline_fp_djibouti_civil = {
	if = { limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_civil FRA = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_civil } }
		clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_civil
		set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_civil value = 1 days = 180 }
		FRA = { clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_civil clr_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_3 set_country_flag = { flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_civil value = 1 days = 180 } }
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_reissue_fp_djibouti_civil = {
	if = { limit = { has_completed_focus = COLDWAR_FRA2_fp_djibouti_civil  NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_cooldown_fp_djibouti_civil } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_signed_fp_global_partnerships_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_family_pending_fp_global_partnerships_3 } NOT = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_pending_fp_djibouti_civil } }
		COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_fp_djibouti_civil = yes
	}
}

COLDWAR_FRA2_sync_treaties = {
	if = {
		limit = { tag = FRA }
			if = {
				limit = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe GER = { has_country_flag = COLDWAR_FRA2_signed_pompidou_europe } COLDWAR_FRA2_offer_active_pompidou_europe = yes }
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france }
				GER = { if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER } } add_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER } }
			}
			else = {
				if = { limit = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france } remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_france }
				if = { limit = { country_exists = GER GER = { has_idea = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER } } GER = { remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_pompidou_europe_GER } }

COLDWAR_FRA2_cleanup_orphaned_recipient_ideas = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = ALG
			NOT = { country_exists = FRA }
		}
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_sovereign_reflation_ALG
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_algeria_energy_ALG
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2_algeria_training_ALG
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			tag = ARG
			NOT = { country_exists = FRA }
		}
		remove_ideas = COLDWAR_FRA2P_fn_export_ARG
	}
